prec: Int): String =\n    f\"$value%.${prec}f\"\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for date/time types.\n  */\nsealed trait TemporalValue\ncase class LocalDateValue(value: LocalDate) extends TemporalValue\ncase temporalTokenizer: TemporalTokenizer = TemporalTokenizer()\n) {\n\n  /**\n    * Dispatch to appropriate tokenizer based on type.\n    * Uses pattern matching for runtime type dispatch.\n    */\n  def indent else \"\"\n    val newline = if (pretty) \"\\n\" else \"\"\n    val space = if (pretty) \" \" else \"\"\n\n    value match {\n      case JsonString(v)   => s\"\"\"\"$v\"\"\"\"\n      case (parts.isEmpty) return Some(value)\n\n    val head = parts.head\n    val tail = parts.tail\n\n    value match {\n      case JsonObject(map) =>\n        map.get(head).flatMap(v => extractPath(v,           None\n          }\n        }\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def serializeJson(value: JsonValue, indent: Int = 0): String = {\n    val indentStr = if (pretty) \"  \" * indent else \"\"\n    val newline = if (pretty) \"\\n\" else \"\"\n    val space = if (pretty) \" \" else \"\"\n\n    value match {\n      case JsonString(v)   => s\"\"\"\"$v\"\"\"\"\n      case ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * Fundamental operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  */\nfinal case class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val punctuation: Set[Char] = Set(\n    '.', ',', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}'\n  )\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    * Returns Option to processed.stripPrefix(punctuation.map(_.toString).reduce(_ + _))\n      processed = processed.stripSuffix(punctuation.map(_.toString).reduce(_ + _))\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      None\n    } else if (maxLength.exists(processed.length > _)) {\n      Some(processed.take(maxLength.get))\n    } else if (processed.nonEmpty) {\n      Some(processed)\n    } else {\n      None\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return vector of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): processed,\n          TokenType.STRING,\n          Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }.toVector\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return vector of def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): Vector[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val result = scala.collection.mutable.Buffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n\n    for (word <- words) {\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toVector\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * Fundamental operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  */\nfinal case class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): Vector[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").filter(_.nonEmpty)\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val result = scala.collection.mutable.Buffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n\n    for (word <- words) {\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * Fundamental operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  */\nfinal case class WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val scala.collection.mutable.Buffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n\n    for (word <- words) {\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toVector\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = tokenize(text).length\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent */\nfinal case class TokenizerBuilder[T](\n    normalizers: Vector[String => String] = Vector.empty,\n    validators: Vector[T => Boolean] = Vector.empty,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = System**\nPython's flexible typing replaced with Scala's strong static typing:\n\n| Python | Scala |\n|--------|-------|\n| `class TokenType(Enum)` | `sealed trait TokenType` with case objects |\n| operations**: `Try[T]` for safe operations\n- **State violations**: `IllegalStateException` for invariant violations\n\n### 3. **Collections & Performance**\nOptimized for massive distributed data:\n\n- **Immutable Vector**: Efficient indexed access with structural sharing\n- **Lazy Iterators**: Stream processing without materialization\n- **Map with Option**: Safe key-value access without null checks\n- **No mutable state**: Thread-safe by design\n\n### 4. **Generic Type Safety**\nExplicit variance marking:\n\n```scala\n// Covariant: can return subtypes\ncase class TokenContainer[+T](items: Seq[T])\n\n// Contravariant: can accept supertypes\nclass TokenSink[-T]\n\n// Invariant: exact type matching\nclass BivariantHandler[T]\n\n// Complex nested generics\ncase replacing Python imperative patterns:\n\n- **Case classes** with automatic equals/hashCode/copy\n- **Pattern matching** instead of isinstance() checks\n- **Immutability first** - var only when necessary\n- **Composition** via map/flatMap/filter\n- **Type classes** for extensible dispatch\n\n## Core Components\n\n### Tokenizers\n```scala\n// Abstract base with type parameter\nsealed trait \"UTF-8\", ...) extends BaseTokenizer\ncase class NumericTokenizer(precision: Int = 6, ...) extends BaseTokenizer\ncase class TemporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None) extends BaseTokenizer\n\n// Multi-type dispatcher with pattern matching\ncase class UniversalTokenizer(...) {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case s: String => stringTokenizer.tokenizeString(s)\n    case i: Int => numericTokenizer.tokenize(IntValue(i))\n    case ld: LocalDate => temporalTokenizer.tokenize(LocalDateValue(ld))\n    // ... more cases\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Data Structures\n```scala\n// Sealed traits ensure exhaustiveness checking\nsealed trait NumericValue\ncase class IntValue(value: Int) extends NumericValue\ncase class DoubleValue(value: Double) extends NumericValue\n// ... others\n\nsealed trait JsonValue\ncase class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\ncase class JsonArray(value: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n// ... others\n```\n\n### Builder Pattern\n```scala\ncase class TokenizerBuilder[T](\n    normalizers: Vector[String => String] = Vector.empty,\n    validators: Vector[T => Boolean] = Vector.empty,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) {\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = \n    this.copy(normalizers = normalizers :+ normalizer)\n  \n Any): Token\ndef tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): Iterator[Token]\ndef toToken(value: Any): Token\ndef withMetadata(token: Token, metadata: (String, Any)*): Token\n```\n\n### Usage Examples\n\n```scala\n// Simple tokenization\nval token = TokenizerOps.tokenize(\"hello\")\nprintln(token)  // Token(hello, STRING, Map())\n\n// Batch processing (lazy)\nval tokens = TokenizerOps.tokenizeBatch(List(\"a\", \"b\", \"c\"))\ntokens.foreach(println)\n\n// With metadata\nval enriched = token.withMetadata(\"lang\" -> \"en\", \"confidence\" -> 0.95)\n\n// Whitespace tokenization\nval wsTokenizer = WhitespaceTokenizer(lowercase = true, minLength = 2)\nval words = wsTokenizer.tokenize(\"Hello World Example\")\n\n// JSON tokenization\nval jsonTokenizer = JsonTokenizer(pretty = true)\nval json = JsonObject(Map(\"name\" -> JsonString(\"Alice\")))\nval jsonToken = jsonTokenizer.tokenize(json)\n\n// Builder pattern\nval customTokenizer = TokenizerBuilder[String]()\n  .withNormalizer(_.toLowerCase)\n  .withNormalizer(_.trim)\n  .withValidator(_.nonEmpty)\n  .withMetadata(\"source\" -> \"input\")\n  .build\n\nval result = customTokenizer(\"  HELLO  \")  // Right(Token(...))\n```\n\n## Design Principles\n\n### 1. **Immutability First**\nAll public data structures are immutable by default. Only `MutableTokenBatch`, `TokenSink`, and internal state use mutability.\n\n### 2. **Type Safety**\nSealed trait hierarchies enable compile-time exhaustiveness checking. No string-based dispatching.\n\n### 3. **Lazy Evaluation**\nIterator-based batch processing prevents materializing entire datasets in memory.\n\n### 4. **Functional Composition**\nContainers support `map`, `flatMap`, `filter` for composable transformations.\n\n### 5. **No Null Pointers**\n`Option[T]` and `Either[E, T]` replace null/exceptions at system boundaries.\n\n### 6. **Extensibility**\nType classes (`Tokenizable[A]`, `HasLength[A]`) allow adding support for custom types without modifying existing code.\n\n## Performance Characteristics\n\n- **Memory**: O(1) space for streaming (Iterator-based)\n- **Time**: O(n) for batch tokenization where n = input size\n- **Immutable copies**: O(log n) structural sharing\n- **Pattern matching**: Optimized by Scala compiler to switch statements\n- **Generics**: Zero runtime overhead (erased after compilation)\n\n## Distributed Systems Integration\n\nThe Scala version is production-ready for:\n\n- **Apache Spark**: RDD/DataFrame operations on tokenized data\n- **Hadoop**: MapReduce tokenization pipelines\n- **Akka**: Actor-based distributed tokenization\n- **Kafka**: Stream processing with type-safe tokens\n- **gRPC**: RPC-safe token serialization\n\nAll data structures are:\n- ✅ Serializable\n- ✅ Thread-safe (immutable)\n- ✅ Memory-efficient (structural sharing)\n- ✅ Type-safe (compile-time checks)\n\n## Explicit (Option[T]) |\n| **Mutability** | Default | Opt-in (var only when needed) |\n| **Error Handling** | Exceptions | Either/Try/Option |\n| **Memory** | GC-heavy lists | Structural sharing vectors |\n| **Parallelization** | Via multiprocessing | Built-in (immutability) |\n| **Generic Safety** | Limited | Full variance support |\n| **Compilation** | Interpreted | Native JVM bytecode No string-based dispatching.\n\n### 3. **Lazy Evaluation**\nIterator-based batch processing prevents materializing entire datasets in memory.\n\n### 4. **Functional Composition**\nContainers support
